Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in dp theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 864 and the invariant I(A,B) = 69 mod 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on dp structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 864. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 69. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^162(S, F) is trivial. QED.
